<script>
            (function (bugsnag) {

                'use strict';

                var readCookie = function (name) {
                    var nameEQ = name + "=";
                    var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
                    for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
                        var c = ca[i];
                        while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);
                        if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
                    }
                    return null;
                };

                try {
                    window.Bugsnag.releaseStage = "Production";
                    var enableExternalPilot = false;
                    var isMobileBetaEnabled = false;

                    

                    if (enableExternalPilot == "true" || isMobileBetaEnabled === true) {
                        window.Bugsnag.releaseStage = 'Pilot';
                    }
                    window.Bugsnag.user = {
                        
                                id: readCookie('profileID'),
                        
                        cart: readCookie('basketID')
                    };

                    window.Bugsnag.metaData = {
                        Hijack: {
                            Referrer: document.referrer,
                            "Is Top Window?": (top.location == location),
                            "User-agent": navigator.userAgent
                        }
                    };
                    Bugsnag.beforeNotify = function (payload) {
                        if (payload && payload.message) {
                            var payloadMessage = payload.message.message || payload.message;

                            if (typeof payloadMessage.match !== 'function') {
                                return true;
                            }

                            var match = payloadMessage.match(/(ppxo.*|fb:like failed to resize in \d+s|\d tags failed to render in \d+ ms|Something went wrong with fetching Twitter hashtags\.)/i);
                            return !(match && match[0].length > 0);
                        } else {
                            return true;
                        }
                    };
                } catch (e) { }

                // Make sure allowed popups only ever open.
                var windowOpen = window.open;
                var allowedPopups = /(?:sandbox.paypal.com)|(?:paypal\.com)|(?:opinionlab\.com)|(?:facebook\.com)|(?:twitter\.com)|(?:pinterest\.com)|(?:google\.com)|(?:smartrecruiters\.com)|(?:kobobooks\.com)|(?:kobobooks\.fr)/i;
                window.open = function (url, name, params) {
                    if (!allowedPopups.test(url || "")) {
                        bugsnag.notify("Possible Hijack", "Url: " + url, {}, "info");
                    }

                    return windowOpen(url, name, params);
                };

                    
                var con = window.console;
                var _warn = con.warn; con.warn = function (msg) { bugsnag.notify('Console Warn', msg, { Details: arguments }, "warn"); _warn.apply(this, arguments); };
                var _error = con.error; con.error = function (msg) { bugsnag.notify('Console Error', msg, { Details: arguments }, "error"); _error.apply(this, arguments); };
                    

            })(window.Bugsnag || {});

<script>
    (function () {
        var publicPreRequireDependencies = {};
        window.prerequire = {
            storage: [],
            registerPublicDependency: function (name, value) {
                publicPreRequireDependencies[name] = value;
            },
            add: function add(dependencies, callback) {
                if (!(dependencies instanceof Array)) {
                    console.error('dependencies should be an Array');
                }
                this.storage.push({ dependencies: dependencies, callback: callback });
            },
            fire: function fire() {
                if (!window.flowFinished) {
                    return;
                }
                for (var i = 0, len = this.storage.length; i < len; i++) {
                    var requirements = this.storage[i];
                    tryCatch(function () {
                        requirements.callback.apply(
                            null,
                            requirements.dependencies.map(function (key, value) {
                                return publicPreRequireDependencies[key];
                            })
                        );
                    }, 'failed to run prerequired callback: ' + requirements.callback)();
                }
            }
        };

        window.tryCatch = function (fn, message) {
            message = message || '';
            if (window.DEBUG) {
                return function () {
                    var args = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments);
                    fn.apply(this, args);
                };
            } else {
                return function () {
                    var errorDetails;
                    var args = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments);
                    try {
                        fn.apply(this, args);
                    } catch (ex) {
                        if (typeof ex === 'string') {
                            errorDetails = ex;
                        } else {
                            errorDetails = {
                                errorType: ex.constructor,
                                message: ex.message,
                                stack: ex.stack
                            };
                        }
                        if (window.Bugsnag) {
                            Bugsnag.notify('Logic Error', message, {
                                ErrorDetails: errorDetails
                            });
                        } else {
                            throw ex;
                        }
                    }
                };
            }
        };
    }());
</script>

Please enable JavaScript before proceeding:</p>
<p style="font: 30px/30px 'Gibson Regular', 'Helvetica', 'Arial', sans-serif; color:#333;">Firefox</p>
<ol style="list-style:decimal inside none; margin: 10px 0 20px;">
<li>On the <strong>Tools</strong> menu (top left of browser), click <strong>Options</strong>.</li>
<li>On the <strong>Content</strong> tab, click to select the <strong>Enable JavaScript</strong> check box.</li>
<li>Click <strong>OK</strong> to close the <strong>Options</strong> popup.</li>
<li><strong>Refresh your browser</strong> page to run scripts and reload content.</li>
</ol>
<p style="font: 30px/30px 'Gibson Regular', 'Helvetica', 'Arial', sans-serif; color:#333;">Internet Explorer</p>
<ol style="list-style:decimal inside none; margin: 10px 0 20px;">
<li>On the <strong>Tools</strong> menu (top right of browser), click <strong>Internet Options</strong>, and then click the <strong>Security</strong> tab.</li>
<li>Click the <strong>Internet Zone</strong>.</li>
<li>If you do not have to customize your Internet security settings, click <strong>Default Level</strong>. Then go to step 5.</li>
<li>If you have to customize your Internet security settings, follow steps a &amp; b, then go to step 5:
        <ol style="list-style:lower-alpha inside none; padding-left: 20px;">
<li>Click <strong>Custom Level</strong>.</li>
<li>In the <strong>Security Settings</strong> - Internet Zone dialog box, click Enable for Active Scripting in the Scripting section.</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li>Click <strong>OK</strong> to close the <strong>Internet Options</strong> popup.</li>
<li><strong>Refresh your browser</strong> page to run scripts and reload content.</li>
</ol></div></noscript></form></body></html>

<p style="font: 30px/30px 'Gibson Regular', 'Helvetica', 'Arial', sans-serif; color:#333;">Chrome</p>
<ol style="list-style:decimal inside none; margin: 10px 0 20px;">
<li>On the <strong>Control</strong> button (top right of browser), select <strong>Settings</strong> from dropdown.</li>
<li>Click on <strong>+ Show advanced settings</strong>.</li>
<li>Under the header <strong>JavaScript</strong> select the following radio button: <strong>Allow all sites to run JavaScript (recommended)</strong>.</li>
<li>Click <strong>OK</strong> to close the <strong>Content Settings</strong> popup and close the <strong>Settings</strong> tab.</li>
<li><strong>Refresh your browser</strong> page to run scripts and reload content.</li>
</ol>
